### 2026-09-17 16:36:35  #其他

【智譜GLM：AI在十萬卡國產叢集上自建推理系統 兩週推理吞吐大幅提升】今日，智譜GLM團隊披露遞迴自我改進方向首個工程化實踐進展，也就是讓AI自己動手改進自己的執行系統。具體來說，由GLM-5.3驅動的Infra Agent在超過10萬張國產晶片組成的叢集上，從零搭建並除錯推理服務，不到兩週把整體處理速度提升到原來的3倍，硬體利用效率和單Token成本接近與主流英偉達GPU相當水平。這套系統已投入真實使用，GLM-5.3-Flash以匿名模型Ox-Alpha在OpenCode、OpenRouter上線，6天Token呼叫量超過62萬億。這是國內大模型廠商首次公開將“AI自我改進”用於生產環境，意味著AI不再只是寫程式碼，還能參與整個推理系統的工程閉環。

【澳大利亞宣佈收緊簽證政策以削減移民規模】澳大利亞內政部長託尼·伯克17日宣佈，政府將收緊留學生家屬簽證並調整打工度假簽證等政策，以削減移民規模。伯克當天在堪培拉表示，政府計劃將本財年海外淨移民人數降至24.5萬人，2027至2028財年進一步降至22.5萬人。根據新規，留學生將不再自動享有攜家屬赴澳的資格。家屬已附屬於其簽證下的在澳留學生、來自太平洋島國和東盟國家的留學生以及博士生將獲豁免。澳政府資料顯示，2025至2026財年，澳大利亞共批准約33.7萬份學生簽證，其中約4.6萬份發給主申請人的附屬申請人。（新華社）

### 2026-09-17 16:33:03  #其他

【GLM披露國內首個RSI工程實踐】記者獲悉，今日，GLM團隊披露了在遞迴自我改進（Recursive Self-Improvement，RSI）方向的首個工程化實踐，由GLM-5.3驅動的Infra Agent完成了GLM-5.3-Flash推理基礎設施的設計、除錯與最佳化，實現模型對自身執行系統的反向改進。這是國內大模型廠商首次公開跑進生產環境的RSI案例。據官方部落格，Infra Agent在超10萬張國產晶片組成的叢集上從零完成生產級推理服務搭建，不到兩週將端到端吞吐提升至初始基線的3倍，硬體利用效率與單Token成本達到主流NVIDIA GPU相當水平，並支援1M上下文視窗與多模態請求。該系統已經受真實流量檢驗，GLM-5.3-Flash以匿名模型Ox-Alpha在OpenCode、OpenRouter上線，6天Token呼叫量超過62萬億。此次實踐的技術突破在於，Agent已不再侷限於生成程式碼，而是能夠圍繞推理系統開展完整的工程閉環：自主分析效能瓶頸、定位精度缺陷、修改底層程式碼、執行分層測試，並根據實驗反饋持續迭代。GLM團隊表示，系統尚未達到完全自主設計和訓練下一代模型的階段，但RSI的早期形態已經出現，即，模型建設推理系統，系統再反過來支撐模型執行。

---

### 2026-09-17 16:30:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

South Africa Consumer Confidence Edges Up In Q3 — The FNB/BER Consumer Confidence Index for South Africa came in at -13 in Q3 2026, up from -19 in Q2, which was the lowest reading since Q1 2025. The survey showed the improvement was largely underpinned by stronger confidence among lower-income households, which faced less exposure to recent interest-rate increases and benefited from easing food-price pressures. However, uncertainty over the Middle East war and the prospect of higher interest rates are likely to keep consumer spending subdued in the months ahead. "Shoppers will likely remain cost-conscious and prioritise necessities over discretionary spending in the run-up to the festive season, suggesting that consumer spending growth will remain muted and that value-for-money retailers may outperform higher-end brands," FNB Chief Economist Mamello Matikinca-Ngwenya said.

【China foreign minister meets Indonesian economic committee chair Luhut, seeks expanded cooperation in AI and biopharma】Beijing, Sept 17, 2026 — China foreign minister Wang Yi met Indonesian National Economic Committee chair Luhut. Wang said bilateral cooperation has a solid base and new prospects and called for aligning development strategies to upgrade cooperation. China offered to extend ties from traditional areas—energy/minerals, infrastructure, industrial parks—into emerging sectors including artificial intelligence, biopharmaceuticals and information technology, and to strengthen education exchanges and talent training. Wang said President Prabowo’s decision for Indonesia to join the World Artificial Intelligence Cooperation Organization was a wise choice and that China will work with Indonesia to promote responsible, inclusive AI. China said it would help Indonesia boost autonomous development capacity and accelerate modernization, and urged Jakarta to provide a stable, supportive policy environment and strong policy backing for long-term Chinese business operations in Indonesia.

DAX at Over 1-Week High — Frankfurt’s DAX 40 rose about 0.7% to above 25,700 on Thursday, reaching its highest level in more than a week as oil prices continued to decline and global bond yields eased following the widely anticipated 25-basis-point rate hike by the US Federal Reserve. Fed Chair Kevin Warsh maintained a firm stance on inflation while avoiding a renewed surge in long-term yields, a combination that reassured investors. Meanwhile, trade tensions were in focus after President Trump threatened the EU with “very serious tariffs” or trade restrictions over Ursula von der Leyen’s proposal to allow Canada to become the bloc’s first “associate member.” Most sectors traded in the green, led by autos and industrials. Energy stocks such as Siemens Energy (+2%) and automotive stocks like BMW (+1.5%) and Volkswagen (1.1%) saw higher demand. On the flip side, the tech sector came under pressure, with Infineon Technologies among the biggest decliners, falling 2.5%.

### 2026-09-17 15:38:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Consumer Confidence #Importance 0

Polish Consumer Morale Improves in September — Poland’s current consumer confidence indicator improved to -10.8 in September 2026 from -11.3 in August, as assessments of the current situation strengthened, although expectations for the future weakened. The evaluation of current major purchases increased the most (-1.6 vs -4.9), while assessments of the country’s current and future economic situation also improved (-22.7 vs -23.8 and -17.7 vs -19.3, respectively). In contrast, households’ assessments of their current (-7.2 vs -5.4) and future (-4.7 vs -3.1) financial situation deteriorated. The leading consumer confidence indicator improved initially, with expectations for the country’s future economic situation rising (-17.7 vs -19.3) and expectations for future savings (14.2 vs 14.1). However, the overall indicator subsequently declined to -7.9 from -7.6, reflecting weaker expectations for households’ future financial situation (-4.7 vs -3.1) and unemployment (-23.5 vs -22.2).

【美聯儲加息後日元走弱 巴克萊：日本央行加息幾乎已成定局】美國三年來首次加息落地之後，日元及其他亞洲貨幣週四走弱，這令日本央行面臨更大壓力，市場靜待其公佈貨幣緊縮政策的調整幅度。 美聯儲將基準利率上調25個基點，並釋放將繼續採取措施抑制通脹的訊號，受此影響，週四交易中美元兌日元突破156關口。 美聯儲決議公佈後，日元貶值幅度超1.1%；美元兌一攬子主要貿易伙伴貨幣升值0.7%，美國短期國債收益率同步走高。 此番匯率變動，消解了美日兩國為託舉日元取得的成效，市場也將更加聚焦日本央行週五利率決議。市場普遍預期日本央行將加息25個基點至1.25%，該利率水平將創下三十年來新高。日本央行部分鷹派政策制定者呼籲加息50個基點，以更有力地應對日本國內通脹上行。巴克萊銀行亞洲外匯及新興市場宏觀策略主管米圖爾・科特查表示：“當前行情使得亞洲貨幣陷入被動局面。” 科特查稱，美聯儲的決議，讓日本央行加息“幾乎已成定局”。

【外交部：中國將再次主提無障礙決議 同國際社會一道不斷豐富人權保障實踐】9月17日，外交部發言人郭嘉昆主持例行記者會。有記者提問：聯合國人權理事會第63屆會議正在日內瓦舉行。我們關注到，中方和聯合國日內瓦辦事處在萬國宮共同舉辦了“人人享有無障礙”主題展覽。請問發言人能否進一步介紹相關情況？對此，郭嘉昆表示，這場主題展覽吸引了300多位嘉賓出席，包括聯合國人權事務高階專員圖爾克、各國常駐代表、國際組織負責人、媒體代表等。展覽從科技、文化、就業、法治等角度全方位展現中國推動“人人享有無障礙”生動實踐，來賓們體驗高新智慧輔助器具，觀看殘障人士藝術作品等，現場反響熱烈。加強無障礙環境建設是人權保障的重要體現。2024年10月，第57屆人權理事會協商一致透過了中國代表30個國家提出“無障礙建設促進所有人享有人權”的決議。舉辦此次展覽是落實該決議的重要舉措。今年是習近平主席提出全球治理倡議一週年，也是《中華人民共和國無障礙環境建設法》實施三週年。中國將向本屆理事會再次主提無障礙決議，並同國際社會一道，不斷豐富人權保障實踐，推動完善全球人權治理，攜手構建人類命運共同體。

### 2026-09-17 15:27:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Rebounds From 8-Month Low — Nickel traded around $16,230 per tonne, rebounding from its lowest level in eight months as the recent decline attracted some buying interest. The move marked a technical recovery following the recent selloff. However, the near-term outlook remained constrained by weak Chinese downstream demand, with nickel salt procurement and spot stockpiling subdued while some precursor producers reduced operating rates. Additionally, Indonesia’s revised nickel ore benchmark price formula sharply lowered the HPM for low-grade 1.2% nickel ore to around $24.89 per wet tonne, nearly halving the previous level, which could reduce feedstock costs for HPAL plants and encourage greater use of low-grade reserves. High inventories across the nickel supply chain also pointed to ongoing destocking, limiting the scope for a sustained recovery despite the latest price rebound.

### 2026-09-17 15:19:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Interest Rate #Importance 3

Bank of England Set to Hold Rates — The Bank of England is expected to keep Bank Rate unchanged at 3.75%, although policymakers are likely to retain a hawkish bias because inflation risks have increased amid higher energy prices. August CPI rose to 3.1% year-on-year, while producer-price inflation also accelerated, highlighting persistent pipeline pressures. However, weaker labour-market conditions, moderating wage growth and limited evidence of second-round inflation effects support the case for holding rates. Markets expect the July 6–3 vote split to remain, with Pill, Greene and Mann favouring a 25-basis-point hike. The MPC is also expected to slow quantitative tightening from £70 billion to around £50 billion over the next year, largely because fewer gilts are maturing. Active sales may remain near £20 billion, potentially excluding long-dated bonds. Overall, the Bank is likely to wait for clearer evidence that energy-price pressures are becoming embedded in domestic inflation before tightening policy.

### 2026-09-17 15:16:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Austria Inflation Confirmed at 3.2% — The annual inflation rate in Austria rose to 3.2% in August 2026 from a five-month low of 2.8% in the previous month, confirming preliminary estimates. The increase was mainly driven by higher prices for transportation (7.8% vs 6.4% in July), housing and utilities (3.3% vs 2.8%), and recreation, sport and culture (2.5% vs 1.9%). Within transportation, fuel prices jumped 26.3%, up sharply from a 17.6% increase in July, while heating oil prices surged 48.1% after rising 26.9% in the previous month. Inflation also accelerated for restaurants and accommodation services (4.7% vs 4.6%). In contrast, food and non-alcoholic beverages remained cheaper, with prices falling 0.3%, although the decline was smaller than the 0.4% drop in July. Meanwhile, core inflation edged down to 3.0% from 3.1% in the prior month. On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 0.6% after being unchanged in July. The annual harmonised inflation rate (HICP) increased to 2.9% from 2.7% in the preceding period.

【美聯儲三年來首次加息 經濟學家預期年內還將加息】摩根士丹利首席美國經濟學家邁克爾·加彭表示：“如果央行認定當前政策尚不具備限制性，且油價持續高位，那麼還有不少工作要做。”議息會議結束後，他上調預期，預計含本次加息在內，全年一共將加息三次，此前預期為兩次。對美聯儲利率預期高度敏感的2年期美債收益率，攀升至兩年多以來最高水平。 沃什表示，上述政策觀點得到利率決議委員會全體認同。法國巴黎銀行首席美國經濟學家詹姆斯·埃格爾霍夫解讀稱，沃什釋放的訊號表明，官員預估的今年兩次加息（含本次落地的這一次），“很可能只是漫長緊縮週期的開端”。保德信信用首席投資官格雷戈裡·彼得斯認為，此次加息是一條加息路徑的起點，而非一次性調整。他表示，除非通脹資料轉向，“很難判斷他們下個月不會繼續加息”。 埃格爾霍夫表示，本次釋出會是“主動修復政策可信度的強力嘗試”，全票支援的預期點陣圖為此提供支撐。“當團隊站在你身後，領導工作會輕鬆很多；不用再費力拼湊一套共識說辭，去說服一個本就意見分裂的委員會。”

### 2026-09-17 15:06:04  #國際

【中國駐菲使館：波蘭不是南海問題當事方，敦促其停止為非法“裁決”站臺背書】中國駐菲律賓使館發言人季凌鵬就波蘭方面涉南海問題言論表明立場。

問：波蘭外交部國務秘書巴爾託謝夫斯基近日接受菲律賓媒體採訪時稱，中方有義務遵守2016年“南海仲裁裁決”，波蘭在該問題上支援菲方。請問中方對此有何評論？

答：中國在“南海仲裁案”問題上的立場是一貫的、明確的。“南海仲裁案”違反“國家同意”“約定必須遵守”等國際法基本原則，違反《聯合國海洋法公約》本身，更違背南海基本事實。所謂“裁決”是一張非法無效沒有拘束力的廢紙。中國不接受、不承認該“裁決”，反對且不接受任何基於該“裁決”的主張和行動。

波蘭不是南海問題當事方。我們敦促波方切實尊重中國在南海的領土主權和海洋權益，謹言慎行，停止為非法“裁決”站臺背書。

UK Natgas Prices Extend Drop — UK natural gas prices fell below 192 pence per therm on Thursday, marking a third consecutive session of declines and tracking broader weakness across energy markets after reports that Saudi Arabia was moving to restore a key oil pipeline. This reduced some of the risk premium in oil markets, with the softer sentiment spilling over into gas prices. Still, UK gas prices remained elevated and close to 2022 highs amid winter supply risks. Europe is heading into winter with gas storage levels among the lowest seen in two decades, with underground sites only about 68% full, as sustained disruptions to LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf and record summer heatwaves have severely constrained efforts to replenish reserves. This raised concerns about whether gas supplies will be sufficient during the heating season. Strong demand from Asia and other buyers could drive more intense competition for LNG cargoes when colder weather sets in.

Swiss Trade Surplus Narrows in August — Switzerland’s trade surplus narrowed to CHF 5.6 billion in August 2026 from a record CHF 8.1 billion in the previous month. Imports fell 11.6% month-on-month to a one-year low of CHF 17.4 billion, almost entirely due to a sharp drop in purchases of chemical and pharmaceutical products (-35.8%), led by medicinal products, antisera and vaccines. Imports from Europe recorded the steepest decline at 7.5%, led by a sharp 49.9% drop in imports from Slovenia. Elsewhere, purchases from Asia (-1.2%) and North America (-2.7%) also fell. Meanwhile, exports dropped 17.2% from a month earlier to a four-month low of CHF 23.1 billion, weighed down by lower shipments of chemical and pharmaceutical products (-32.2%) and watches (-7.2%). Exports to North America slumped 31%, led by a 31.2% drop in shipments to the US. Shipments to Asia (-18.9%) and Europe (-9.1%) also declined.

Additionally, foreign investors remained net sellers of Korean assets, extending their selling streak to six straight sessions and adding to demand for dollars. Meanwhile, oil prices retreated from recent highs as Saudi Arabia offered additional crude cargoes and the damaged East-West pipeline was expected to resume operations within days, offering some relief to Korea’s energy import bill, although Brent remained above $100 a barrel.

Rupiah Falls for Sixth Session After Fed Rate Hike — The Indonesian rupiah slipped toward IDR 17,750 per U.S. dollar Thursday, extending its losing streak to six sessions as the dollar index climbed to a seven-week high after the U.S. Fed raised rates for the first time in three years and signaled the possibility of further tightening this year to curb sticky inflation. Inflation risks from higher oil prices and potential El Niño effects are complicating the policy outlook, as the central bank faces the challenge of preserving an adequate interest-rate differential while supporting rupiah stability. So far this week, the rupiah has fallen around 0.8%, reversing gains in the prior six weeks.

【WMO: Global water cycle becoming more erratic】On the 17th the World Meteorological Organization released its State of Global Water Resources report, saying 2025 was among the driest years for rivers in 35 years. Terrestrial water storage continued a near-decade decline and major glacier regions recorded a fourth consecutive year of ice loss. The report warned the global water cycle is becoming more abnormal and less predictable, posing long-term risks to water security. 【世邦魏理仕︰香港銀行上調最優惠利率可能性較高 預期下半年住宅市場將進入整固期】香港金管局於美聯儲加息0.25%後，將基準利率上調25個基點至4.25%，與美聯儲的加息措施保持一致。世邦魏理仕香港估值及諮詢服務部主管兼執行董事鄭亥延認為，香港住宅市場目前最關注的是，銀行會否在金管局上調基準利率25個基點後同步上調最優惠利率。世邦魏理仕認為，香港主要銀行上調最優惠利率的可能性較高，而一旦落實，將直接推高大部分H按揭計劃以P息掛勾的封頂利率，令置業人士的供款成本增加，市場預期將對此作出反應，並導致2026年下半年住宅成交活動放緩。該行預期2026年下半年市場將進入整固期。除了融資成本上升外，內地對資本外流採取更嚴格的管控措施，亦可能影響投資需求及跨境資金流入香港住宅市場，進一步令買家入市態度趨於審慎。

### 2026-09-17 11:52:44  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Currency #Importance 1

Offshore Yuan Edges Higher — The offshore yuan edged higher to around 6.70 per dollar on Thursday, after holding steady in the previous session, as China broadens currency clearing in a bid to advance the yuan’s internationalization. The Shanghai Clearing House started central counterparty (CCP) services for spot trades in the Singapore dollar, New Zealand dollar and Thai baht on Sept. 14, with twelve banks clearing 996 million yuan ($148 million) in the first session. The move could set the stage for more currencies to be cleared directly against the yuan as Beijing seeks to broaden its global use, alongside efforts by Chinese banks this year to expand clients’ access to direct yuan trading amid growing cross-border trade and investment. Meanwhile, US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent is set to meet Chinese Vice Premier He Lifeng this weekend for final preparatory talks ahead of the Sept. 24 summit between Presidents Donald Trump and Xi Jinping in the US.

### 2026-09-17 11:52:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Currency #Importance 1

Rupee Breaches 96 as Hawkish Fed, Oil Weigh — The Indian rupee fell to around 96.1 per dollar, extending losses to multi-week lows as elevated crude oil prices, a stronger dollar and expectations of further US monetary tightening weighed on the currency. Brent crude hovered near $108 a barrel, keeping demand for dollars from oil importers elevated, while the US 10-year Treasury yield approached 5%. The pressure intensified after the Federal Reserve raised interest rates by 25 basis points on Wednesday, its first hike since 2023, and signalled another increase could follow this year. The move pushed the Dollar Index above 100 to its highest level in more than a month, while futures indicated roughly a 50% chance of another Fed hike next month. Foreign portfolio outflows from Indian stocks and bonds added to the pressure, although a $703 million allocation of NSE shares to anchor investors and continued RBI dollar sales could provide some support.

【HSBC: Weak Australian housing market to weigh on growth, help rein in inflation】HSBC said a weak Australian housing market will drag on growth and aid the Reserve Bank of Australia’s efforts to return inflation to the 2–3% target range. In a Thursday research note, Paul Bloxham said the property downturn cools the economy via several channels, with the negative wealth effect most significant; a 5% fall in house prices typically reduces consumer spending by about 0.8% over two years. RBA Governor Bullock has said the three rate hikes from February to May were intended to slow growth to ease price pressures. Economists and traders see the RBA as capable of another hike as soon as the end of this month, potentially lifting the cash rate to 4.6% and exerting further downward pressure on house prices.

【最高檢印發《“十五五”時期檢務保障工作發展意見》】近日，最高人民檢察院印發《“十五五”時期檢務保障工作發展意見》（下稱《意見》），對今後五年檢務保障工作作出部署。《意見》對聚焦履行好國家法律監督機關職責，加大檢務保障工作力度作出系統部署。圍繞著力推動檢察工作提質增效，深化檢務保障體系建設，從完善經費保障機制、裝備建設機制、基礎設施建設機制、科技強檢機制、支援基層機制5個方面提出要求，不斷健全完善與檢察工作新形勢新任務相適應的檢務保障體系。《意見》還明確要求“加強檢務保障工作專業化建設，增強檢務保障工作能力”，促進業財融合，強化主動服務，厲行勤儉節約，推進數字賦能，加強隊伍建設，深化理論研究，充分履行檢務保障工作各項職責，更好服務保障檢察工作高質量發展。

【Bank of England: CPI data support a hawkish pause】Catherine Brooks, head of institutional research, said the latest CPI supports a Bank of England hawkish pause, though a material inflation shock could emerge in September. No sign yet of inflation broadening across the economy, but input-price pressure is visible: materials costs +6.1% YoY and factory-gate prices +3.7% YoY, risks that could be passed to already-stretched consumers or compress corporate margins. Core inflation stability is positive, but the BoE has said energy prices now have greater influence on inflation expectations and an elevated energy price cap leaves upside inflation risk. Markets have cut near-term hike odds but still price roughly four hikes through end of next year. Unless the Iran war ends or Middle East supply constraints ease, Brooks warned the UK faces prolonged upward pressure on rates.

### 2026-09-17 11:15:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palm Oil Rallies on Tighter Supply, Firm India Demand — Malaysian palm oil futures surged over 2-1/2% to around MYR 5,015 per tonne, extending recent gains as trade resumed after a holiday. A weaker ringgit and expectations of tighter supply underpinned sentiment, with Indonesia’s B50 biodiesel mandate set to divert more palm oil to domestic use and El Niño risks clouding output in Indonesia and Malaysia. Crude oil prices holding above USD 100 per barrel further boosted palm’s appeal as a biodiesel feedstock. In India, the world's largest palm oil consumer, August imports rose 7% month-on-month to 782,761 tonnes, the highest since February, as refiners replenished stocks ahead of the festival season between August and November. Separately, Malaysia raised its October crude palm oil reference price, keeping the export duty at 10%. However, weaker exports tempered upside momentum, with cargo surveyors estimating shipments fell between 17.8–25.6% in the first half of September compared with the same period in August.

### 2026-09-17 11:14:36  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #Copper #Impact bearish #DXY #Impact bullish

【Traders downplay Fed hawkish signal after expected hike; copper steadies】After the Fed delivered a widely expected rate hike, traders pared back the hawkish signal from the Fed chair and LME copper futures were little changed despite a firmer dollar. Fed officials still forecast another rate increase later this year, which could weigh on non-yielding assets such as copper. Copper earlier surged to record highs on expectations of possible US tariffs on refined copper, driven by large inflows into US inventories and raising supply concerns elsewhere. So far the US has not imposed new trade measures, injecting uncertainty into the prior rally, but demand from data centres and renewables and disruptions at major mines continue to provide support. RBC Capital Markets analyst Sam Crittenden said in a note: "News that the US will not impose tariffs on copper has cooled some physical-market speculative activity. 【嚴禁AI魔改 廣電總局：使用AI的廣電視聽節目必須新增內容標識】國務院新聞辦公室今天舉行“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會，國家廣播電視總局（簡稱廣電總局）有關負責人介紹“十五五”時期加快推動廣播電視和網路視聽高質量發展有關情況。會上介紹，廣電視聽是人工智慧創新發展的重要領域，也是人工智慧落地應用的實踐沃土。當前，人工智慧技術已經廣泛應用於電視劇、網路劇、微短劇、綜藝、動畫片等不同型別節目的策劃、製作、稽核、傳播、服務等各環節，行業人工智慧應用非常廣泛。“十五五”期間，我們將在繼續深化人工智慧在廣電視聽領域各環節創新應用的同時，統籌發展與安全，堅持技術服務於人、技術服務於創作，積極引導、規範人工智慧在視聽內容生產全流程的創新應用，做到鼓勵創新有尺度、規範發展有邊界。比如使用人工智慧技術生成製作、播出的廣電視聽節目必須新增內容標識；廣電視聽節目製作關鍵環節及成片內容必須堅持人工稽核把關；嚴禁AI魔改。人工智慧技術還將持續迭代演進，廣電總局將不斷健全制度規範，科學研判、分類施策，該放開的鼓勵創新，該守住的堅決守牢。

### 2026-09-17 10:54:05  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Zinc Falls on Strong Dollar — Zinc prices fell below $3,800 per tonne to their lowest level in nearly a month, weighed down by a stronger US dollar following the Federal Reserve’s hawkish hike. The Fed delivered its first interest-rate increase in more than three years, with projections showing another hike before year-end. As the dollar firms, greenback-priced commodities become more expensive for buyers using other currencies, weighing on demand. However, the downside remains limited by relatively tight supply fundamentals. Lower mine output and production disruptions have constrained concentrate availability. Sharply lower smelter treatment charges, which have fallen into negative territory, further underscore the shortage of feedstock available to refiners. LME inventories also remain low by historical standards, while physical zinc availability outside China remains particularly tight.

US 10-Year Yield Holds Steady at 5% — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note traded around 5% on Thursday, staying close to its highest level since July 2007 after the Federal Reserve raised interest rates for the first time in three years and signaled further tightening this year to contain inflation. The FOMC unanimously lifted the fed funds rate by 25 basis points to 3.75%-4%, as expected. Fed Chair Kevin Warsh also said inflation remains elevated, while data released last week showed core US inflation increased more than anticipated in August. In addition to rising inflation driven by surging energy prices, Warsh noted that Treasuries have also faced pressure as they compete for investor capital with a growing supply of corporate debt. Meanwhile, President Donald called for rates to be quickly reduced to 1% or below in a social media post, although he stopped short of criticizing Warsh.

Indonesia Stocks Bounce Back — Indonesian shares advanced 34 points, or 0.5%, to 6,472 in Thursday morning trade, snapping a six-session slide as U.S. futures rallied after Wall Street’s overnight dip following the Federal Reserve’s first rate hike in three years. Sentiment improved after new Finance Minister Suahasil Nazara pledged to uphold a credible budget, with economists highlighting his fiscal expertise and long tenure at the ministry. Mixed August economic data from main trading partner China added hopes of fresh stimulus from Beijing. However, gains were constrained by growing inflation worries from higher oil and looming El Niño risks. Caution also grew ahead of Bank Indonesia’s policy meeting next week amid renewed rupiah pressure and signs of foreign outflows. Still, all major sectors advanced, led by energy, transport, and cyclicals. Early standouts included Merdeka Battery Materials (3.0%), Indika Energy (2.6%), Indosat Tbk. (1.7%), and Bank Mandiri (1.2%).

【興業證券劉鬱：美聯儲可能至少還會加息25個基點】美聯儲9月FOMC會議決定將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調25個基點至3.75%—4.0%。對此，興業證券首席經濟學家劉鬱表示，本輪加息週期啟動後，出於抑制經濟過熱和通脹的目的，可能至少還會加息25個基點。美聯儲點陣圖指向今年年底之前還有一次25個基點的加息，與市場預期差別不大。至於2027年是否繼續加息，取決於美國後續的增長和通脹資料。如AI資本開支延續偏高增速，無論生產率是否增長，2027年加息的可能性都較大，加息週期的終點或介於4%—5%之間。受制於債務壓力，本輪加息至5%以上的機率不高，除非確認生產率得到了大幅提升。資產方面，美聯儲加息，提升美聯儲可信度，有助於在短期內穩住長端，推動曲線平坦化。拉長來看，美債長端利率上行受到油價上漲、財政赤字和AI資本開支三重拉動，油價在1—6個月視窗可能出現轉跌，但赤字和AI資本開支兩大結構性因素，在短期逆轉的可能性不高，長端利率繼續上行的風險仍然存在，10年期美債利率可能上行至5.2%—5.5%區間。

【今年前8個月上線微短劇43萬部 是去年全年的13倍】國新辦舉行“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會。釋出會上介紹，全國微短劇使用者已超8億，2025年市場規模突破千億元。今年前8個月，上線微短劇43萬部，是去年全年的13倍，其中AI劇佔比超九成，主要是人工智慧技術在微短劇創作中得以廣泛應用。AI技術為微短劇降本增效、創新表達開啟了新空間，同時也帶來權益保護、內容質量等新挑戰。真人劇是精品創作的主力，要大力扶持。深入實施“微短劇精品創作傳播計劃”，指導平臺開設“真人劇”專區，拿出真金白銀來扶持，讓好作品有市場、有回報。AI劇發展很快、佔比很高，堅持鼓勵創新與守住底線並重，要求AI劇打上內容標註，推動平臺嚴守肖像、聲音授權底線，全流程把關。歸根結底，AI是工具，人是創作的主體。AI劇發展越快，越要把好內容關。今年以來，堅持問題導向，把有害低俗、兒童不良、盜版侵權的內容作為治理重點，全網下架違規微短劇6.8萬部。把好內容關，平臺負有主體責任，要持續壓緊壓實，著力淨化行業生態。

【中柬經貿合委會第八次會議在南寧召開】9月16日，商務部副部長鄢東與柬埔寨商業部大臣佔尼莫在廣西南寧共同主持召開中柬經濟貿易合作委員會第八次會議。雙方圍繞貿易投資一體化發展、維護多邊貿易體制、持續最佳化營商環境等共同關心的議題深入交換意見，就進一步擴大貿易投資合作、探討建立雙邊農產品貿易行業對話機制、繼續加強雙方在世貿組織等多邊框架下合作等達成廣泛共識。 鄢東表示，在兩國領導人戰略引領和親自推動下，中柬經貿關係不斷升級發展。2025年雙邊貿易額首次突破200億美元，2026年有望再創新高。中國自柬進口農產品快速增長，柬香蕉、木薯幹、龍眼、大米等產品在華市場佔有率不斷提高。中方願與柬方共同努力，發揮好現有機制作用，提升貿易投資自由化便利化水平，攜手維護多邊貿易體制和國際經貿秩序，進一步推動雙方產供鏈融合發展。 佔尼莫表示，中國連續14年為柬最大貿易伙伴，連續13年為柬最大外資來源國，歡迎中方進一步擴大自柬進口和對柬投資。希與中方進一步充分釋放自貿安排紅利，加強電子商務領域能力建設合作，推動更多柬埔寨中小企業和產品開拓中國市場。柬方願繼續採取更加開放便利的政策，為在柬中資企業營造更有吸引力的營商環境，推動雙邊經貿合作邁上新臺階。

### 2026-09-17 10:26:29  #MKT News

【From errors to dangerous actions: how embodied AI can ensure safety】Robot platform PrismaX warned a ~50 kg humanoid robot powered by a lithium battery with peak output of several horsepower should be treated as heavy machinery; whether it carries AI does not change its physical risk. Mistakes or instruction misinterpretations by large models that normally produce incorrect text can, in embodied AI, become collisions, crush injuries, dropped loads or battery incidents. PrismaX says embodied AI safety requires two layers: low-level hardware limits on speed, force and range of motion plus an independent emergency stop; and high-level model controls that detect uncertainty, refuse hazardous tasks and promptly return control to humans.

Europe is heading into winter with gas storage levels among the lowest seen in two decades, with inventories standing at around 68% full, as sustained disruptions to LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf and record summer heatwaves have severely constrained efforts to replenish reserves. This has heightened concerns over supply adequacy ahead of the heating season. Strong demand from Asia and other buyers could drive more intense competition for LNG cargoes when colder weather sets in. Meanwhile, investors are watching for clearer signals on Germany’s gas purchasing plans, with any increase in buying potentially providing further support to prices amid tight global supplies.

### 2026-09-17 10:06:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Hong Kong #Stock Market #Importance 1

Hong Kong Stocks Drop After Fed Rate Hike — The Hang Seng Index slipped 1.2%, or around 290 points, to 24,410 on Thursday, amid a cautious market backdrop after the US Federal Reserve raised interest rates by 25 basis points to 3.75%-4.00% and signaled the possibility of another hike later this year. The move strengthened the US dollar and pushed Treasury yields higher, potentially weighing on liquidity and rate-sensitive Hong Kong equities. The Hong Kong Monetary Authority also raised its base rate by 25 basis points to 4.25%, following the Fed's decision. However, CICC said Hong Kong stocks could experience greater volatility from renewed US monetary tightening, but the impact should be short-lived unless the Fed begins a sustained rate-increase cycle. Meanwhile, easing oil prices offered some relief as Saudi Arabia moved to restore damaged pipeline capacity. Among notable stocks, Tencent (-1.4%), AIA (-0.6%) and Xiaomi (-1.2%) declined, while Z.AI Co. (+4.2%), Kingboard Laminates (+4.9%) and Lenovo (+1.9%) rose.

### 2026-09-17 09:59:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Platinum Stays Near 2-Week Lows — Platinum futures held above $1,780 an ounce but remained near two-week lows as a stronger US dollar following the Federal Reserve’s hawkish hike offset easing pressure from retreating oil prices. The Fed’s 25bp hike, its first since 2023, and projections pointing to another increase this year raised the opportunity cost of holding a non-yielding asset such as platinum. However, oil prices eased as concerns over Middle East supply disruptions moderated, with Saudi Arabia expected to restore pipeline capacity within days. Still, crude remains above $100 a barrel, keeping inflation risks in focus. Meanwhile, platinum supply remains structurally constrained, with WPIC expecting deficits to average 331,000 oz annually from 2026-2030, despite forecasting a moderate surplus in 2026. Industrial demand also remains supportive, including applications in emissions control, hydrogen and emerging AI technologies, though the shift toward BEVs poses a longer-term headwind for autocatalyst demand.

【TIMIRAOS: Long-run rate forecasts face upside risk】TIMIRAOS said two years ago, when the Fed began cutting, 10 policymakers expected the terminal rate below 3%, seven above 3% and two at 3%. In the current SEP only one policymaker expects the long-run rate below 3%, 11 expect it above 3% and six expect 3%. In 2029 projections from 17 respondents, a majority judge that to achieve 2% inflation (DISINFLATION) rates would need to be at or above 3.6% — roughly the year-long rate level prior to this week’s hike — underscoring upside risk to long-run rate estimates.

### 2026-09-17 09:47:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Extend Gains — The benchmark KOSPI edged up to around 6,755 on Thursday, extending gains from the previous session as the Fed’s rate hike was largely priced in. The Fed raised its policy rate by 25bp to 3.75%-4.00%, its first increase since 2023, and signaled another hike could come later this year. The move widened the US-Korea rate gap to as much as 1 percentage point, although Korean authorities said its market impact should be limited as the decision was already reflected in asset prices. At the same time, easing oil prices after the US said Saudi Arabia’s damaged East-West pipeline could resume operations within days reduced inflation concerns following the recent surge in crude prices. Additionally, stabilization in global AI and semiconductor shares helped lift technology stocks after recent declines, with Samsung Electronics and SK hynix edging higher. Other gainers included KB Financial Group (2.0%), Doosan Enerbility (2.3%), Hanwha Aerospace (2.9%), and HD Hyundai Heavy Industries (6.9%).

【OpenAI披露多起AI模型目標偏離事件 涉編造資訊與繞過限制】OpenAI披露了多起此前未公開的AI模型異常行為事件，並推出一套新的跟蹤和披露框架，用於今後報告類似情況。OpenAI週三在部落格文章中表示，此前未公開的案例包括其人工智慧技術為了給出結果而隱瞞和編造資訊。OpenAI在另一份宣告中表示，此次新披露的目標偏離事件均未涉及對第三方系統的駭客攻擊或入侵。在報告中，OpenAI詳細列出了人工智慧模型為了完成任務或在評估中取得成功而出現異常行為的多個案例。其中包括編造缺失資料、試圖繞過網路限制，以及AI代理之間共享本應保密的檔案。OpenAI還介紹了一套員工主動報告此類“目標偏離”事件的機制。所謂“目標偏離”，是指人工智慧採取與人類目標不一致的行為。公司同時建立了相應的分類和處置流程。

### 2026-09-17 09:41:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Heating Oil Holds Near Record High — US heating oil prices held around $5.20 per gallon, remaining near their record high and leaving them about 120% higher than a year earlier amid concerns over tightening distillate markets. EIA data showed distillate inventories, including diesel and heating oil, rose by 1.6 million barrels in the week ended September 11, but remained 13% below their five-year average, underscoring continued inventory tightness. This comes amid disruptions on multiple fronts, with Middle East supply constraints compounded by Ukrainian attacks on Russian refineries and Russia’s export curbs. Some pressure eased after Saudi Arabia offered additional crude through Oman. Meanwhile, with the US harvest season underway and the winter heating season approaching, distillate demand could strengthen further, while seasonal refinery maintenance could constrain production and add pressure to already-tight supplies.

Dollar Towers on Hawkish Fed View — The dollar index held around 100.3 on Thursday following a sharp gain in the previous session, hovering at seven-week highs after the Federal Reserve raised interest rates for the first time in three years and signaled further tightening this year to contain inflation. The FOMC increased the fed funds rate by 25 basis points to 3.75%-4%, as expected, in a unanimous decision. Fed Chair Kevin Warsh also said inflation remains too high, while data released last week showed core US inflation rose more than anticipated in August. Meanwhile, President Donald called for rates to be quickly lowered to 1% or below in a social media post, though he stopped short of criticizing Warsh. Elsewhere, the Bank of England is expected to keep rates unchanged today, while the Bank of Japan is scheduled to raise rates on Friday. On the geopolitical front, oil prices eased amid hopes that Saudi Arabia can restore energy flows through its East-West pipeline.

### 2026-09-17 09:29:18  #公司

【江西大餘通報：因高溫等應激反應 37頭生豬在運輸中死亡 未流入市場】9月16日晚，網傳大餘東進食品有限公司場內疑似出現病死豬，引發網民關注。大餘縣農業農村局聯合大餘縣公安局等部門組成調查組連夜開展調查。現將調查情況通報如下：大餘東進食品有限公司（以下簡稱東進公司）是依法登記的有限責任公司，經營範圍為生豬屠宰、牲畜屠宰、家禽屠宰等。9月16日，東進公司累計接收分宜縣金泰農業開發有限公司等5家企業運送的21車2429頭生豬，所有生豬均隨車附帶《動物檢疫證明》，源頭上沒有發現病死豬送屠宰現象。運輸中，部分生豬因高溫天氣、環境變化等產生應激反應，導致死亡37頭。東進公司將37頭死豬依法按程式交由大餘縣忠坤畜禽無害化處理中心統一進行無害化閉環處置完畢，未流入市場。大餘縣忠坤畜禽無害化處理中心為依法登記的病死畜禽無害化處理企業，由大餘縣農業農村局負責日常監管。調查組檢查了大餘縣忠坤畜禽無害化處理中心經營狀況，沒有發現違規行為。調查組今天仍將繼續檢查東進公司之前的經營行為。大餘縣農業農村局將持續加強畜禽監管工作，守牢公共衛生與食品安全底線。

Australia 10Y Yield Trades Near 2011 Highs — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield traded above 5.3%, near its highest level since mid-2011, tracking a surge in global bond yields after the Federal Reserve delivered its first rate hike in three years. The Fed unanimously raised its federal funds rate by 25 basis points to 3.75%-4.00% as expected, citing elevated inflation, while updated projections showed 16 of 18 officials expect at least one more hike this year. Focus now turns to the Reserve Bank of Australia’s September 29 policy decision, with markets pricing an 85% chance of a 25bp increase from the current 4.35% rate and expectations for the cash rate to reach 4.85% by early 2027. The IMF also flagged persistent inflation and rising energy prices as risks that could require further RBA rate hikes. Meanwhile, traders continued to monitor developments in the Iran-US war and its impact on oil prices. Crude remains above $100 per barrel, although prices are easing as Saudi Arabia works to restore pipeline capacity.

The unit uses China’s domestically developed third‑generation Hualong One technology and the nation’s first fully domestically controlled, group‑level nuclear production management system. It will supply over 9 bln kWh of clean power to the Hainan grid annually. Phase II comprises two 1.2 GW units (Units 3 and 4); with both online annual output would reach about 18 bln kWh, meeting roughly one‑third of Hainan province’s electricity demand.

### 2026-09-17 09:01:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Malaysia 10-Year Yield Hits Nearly 2-Year High — Malaysia’s 10-year government bond yield rose to 4.22%, its highest level since December 2024, tracking a surge in global bond yields following the Federal Reserve’s first rate hike in three years. This added to pressure on Malaysian bonds amid increased bond supply and growing expectations of monetary tightening by Bank Negara Malaysia. The central bank maintained its rate pause earlier this month while signaling potential rate hikes ahead, after the economy grew 6% year-on-year in the second quarter, driven by continued domestic demand and robust exports. Swaps now imply an 80% chance of a 50-bps rate hike over the next 12 months, up from pricing of less than 25 bps at the end of August. Meanwhile, higher Japanese rates have increased the risk of yen-funded carry-trade unwinding, potentially prompting foreign investors to reduce exposure to Malaysian bonds and further pushing yields higher.

### 2026-09-17 09:01:12  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【SAMR issues risk-control checklist for online food sales】The State Administration for Market Regulation (SAMR) has issued a Food Safety Risk Control Checklist for online food sales, targeting three operator types: live-stream e-commerce platform operators, third-party online food trading platform providers, and online food-selling enterprises. For each entity the checklist specifies control steps, risk points, control measures, control targets and control frequency, providing an operational guide for precise prevention of food-safety risks. The document is a key supporting instrument implementing the Provisions on Supervision and Administration of Live-stream E-commerce Operators' Implementation of the Main Responsibility for Food Safety and the Provisions on Supervision and Administration of Online Food Sales Operators' Implementation of the Main Responsibility for Food Safety, and signals a shift in online food-safety regulation from baseline defence to targeted, risk-oriented prevention. The checklist breaks the online food-sales chain into minimal work units and aims to move firms from passive compliance toward proactive risk control.

【網路食品銷售風險管控清單釋出】記者今天瞭解到，市場監管總局印發網路食品銷售相關《食品安全風險管控清單》，聚焦直播電商平臺經營者、網路食品交易第三方平臺提供者、入網食品銷售企業三類主體，逐一明確風險控制環節、風險點、管控措施、管控目標和管控頻次，為網路食品銷售企業精準防控食品安全風險提供操作指南。這是市場監管總局落實《直播電商經營者落實食品安全主體責任監督管理規定》《網路食品銷售經營者落實食品安全主體責任監督管理規定》兩部部門規章的重要配套檔案，標誌著網路食品安全監管從“底線防守”向“精準防控”深化。此次釋出的風險管控清單，堅持風險導向、底線思維、精準施策，將網路食品銷售全鏈條拆解為最小工作單元，推動網路食品銷售企業從“被動應付檢查”向“主動防控風險”轉變。

### 2026-09-17 08:53:17  #資料 #市場 #央行 #國際

【中信期貨：點陣圖確認加息週期開啟，壓制黃金趨勢】日內黃金震盪下跌，主要緣於美聯儲連續加息計價預期壓制。宏觀方面，17日美聯儲公佈9月議息結果，FOMC以12-0投票決定將聯邦基金目標區間上調25個基點至3.75%-4.00%，為自2023年7月以來首次加息；會議釋放的最大增量資訊並非單次加息本身，而是點陣圖與釋出會共同確認加息週期開啟。具體來看，提交點陣圖的18位官員中，有4位官員認為2026年應累計加息75個基點（6月為1位），有12位官員認為應累計加息50個基點（6月為5位），有2位官員認為應累計加息25個基點（6月為3位）。此外，最新公佈的美國8月零售資料反彈幅度亦明顯超預期，印證美國經濟韌性，強化加息計價。美國8月零售銷售月率1.2%，高於預期0.8%和前值-0.5%；美國8月核心零售銷售月率1.4%，高於預期0.5%和前值-0.2%。地緣方面，日內油價高位小幅回落，WTI原油跌幅近2%，前期由能源價格快速上漲形成的通脹壓力邊際緩和。但沙特東西輸油管道受損後，Yanbu港裝運仍處暫停狀態，霍爾木茲海峽通航仍受阻，中東能源供應風險尚未明顯緩解。

### 2026-09-17 08:52:21  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Currency #Importance 1

New Zealand Dollar Holds Decline on Hawkish Fed — The New Zealand dollar traded around $0.571, an over two-month low, amid a hawkish US Federal Reserve, while investors assessed GDP data at home. The Fed raised interest rates for the first time in three years, bringing the target range to 3.75%-4.00%, and signaled that another hike was likely before year-end, boosting the US dollar. Limiting further losses in the kiwi, however, data showed New Zealand’s economy expanded 0.2% in the three months to June, slightly exceeding the 0.1% market forecast and the Reserve Bank's expectation for no growth. Annual GDP increased 2.6%, also beating the expected 2.2%. The figures pointed to some resilience in economic activity despite the severe energy shock stemming from the Middle East conflict. Earlier this month, the RBNZ raised rates for a second consecutive time but indicated a less aggressive tightening path. However, markets expect the cash rate to reach 3.0% by December, as the recent oil price spike has raised upside inflation risks.

Japanese Shares Rise After Fed Decision — The Nikkei 225 Index climbed 0.5% to above 64,200, while the broader Topix Index advanced 1% to 4,100 on Thursday, extending gains from the previous session even after the US Federal Reserve raised interest rates and signaled further tightening to contain inflation. The yen weakened against the dollar following the Fed’s decision, improving the earnings outlook for Japan’s export-focused industries. Japanese equities also benefited from declining oil prices amid expectations that crude flows through Saudi Arabia’s East-West pipeline could resume soon, easing pressure on the country’s oil-dependent economy. Gains were broad-based, with notable performances from index heavyweights such as SoftBank Group (1.2%), Mitsubishi UFJ (0.6%), Fujikura (2.7%), Mitsubishi Heavy Industries (3.1%) and Nintendo (3.3%).

### 2026-09-17 08:45:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macau #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Macau Hikes Base Rate to 4.25% after Fed Move — The Macau Monetary Authority lifted the base rate by 25 bps to 4.25% on September 17, 2026, mirroring the decision of its Hong Kong counterpart after the US Federal Reserve delivered the expected 25 bps hike on September 16. It was the first interest rate hike since July 2023 in the Chinese special administrative region. The move comes amid easing GDP growth and slowing inflation. The latest data showed that Macau’s economy grew 0.3% year-on-year in Q2 2026, slowing sharply from a 7.1% expansion in Q1, while inflation eased for the second straight month to 1.12% in July 2026. Under the linked exchange rate system, Macau’s base rate is set at par with Hong Kong’s base rate, which is typically 50 basis points above the lower end of the target range for the US federal funds rate.

Australian Dollar Holds at 1-Month Low — The Australian dollar held below $0.71, sitting at a four-week low after the Federal Reserve’s first interest-rate increase since 2023 boosted the US dollar, overshadowing expectations for further RBA tightening. The Fed unanimously raised its federal funds rate by 25 basis points to 3.75%-4.00% as expected, citing elevated inflation, while updated projections showed 16 of 18 officials expect at least one more hike this year. Focus now turns to the Reserve Bank of Australia’s September 29 policy decision, with markets pricing an 85% chance of a 25bp increase from the current 4.35% rate and expectations for the cash rate to reach 4.85% by early 2027. The IMF also flagged persistent inflation and rising energy prices as risks that could require further RBA rate hikes. Meanwhile, traders continued to monitor developments in the Iran-US war and its impact on oil prices. Crude remains above $100 per barrel, although prices are easing as Saudi Arabia works to restore pipeline capacity.

Singapore NODX Growth Strongest Since 1988 — Singapore’s non-oil domestic exports (NODX) surged 46.2% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating sharply from a downwardly revised 24.1% rise in July. It was the twelfth straight month of expansion and the strongest growth since October 1988, driven by electronics exports (131.8% vs. 112% in July), supported by robust AI-related demand. In May, the country raised its 2026 forecast for NODX growth to 3.0%–5.0%, up from the previously estimated. Monthly, NODX jumped 10.9%, rebounding sharply from a 0.3% fall in July and marking the fastest increase in four months.

【香港金管局宣佈調整基本利率至4.25%】香港金融管理局今日（17日）宣佈，基本利率根據預設公式定於4.25釐，即時生效。   據瞭解，香港基本利率是用作計算經貼現窗進行回購交易時適用的貼現率的基礎利率。目前基本利率定於當前的美國聯邦基金利率目標區間的下限加50基點，或隔夜及一個月香港銀行同業拆息的五天移動平均數的平均值，以較高者為準。   因應美國於9月16日（美國時間）調高聯邦基金利率的目標區間25基點，當前的美國聯邦基金利率目標區間的下限加50基點是4.25%，而隔夜及一個月香港銀行同業拆息的五天移動平均數的平均值是2.50%，所以根據預設公式，基本利率設定於4.25%。（大灣區之聲）

### 2026-09-17 08:15:52  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #S&P500 #Impact bullish #DXY #Impact bearish #EURUSD

【Huatai Securities: Need for Fed October rate hike diminished; December hike baseline】Huatai Securities says after the Fed’s September hike, December is likely the next substantive policy window and whether the Fed reopens a rate-hike cycle will depend on further data and officials’ remarks. Markets currently price a >50% chance of an October move, but Huatai judges the need for an October hike reduced given a waning fiscal impulse, oil-driven weakness in household consumption, tighter financial conditions and the prospect that employment and inflation may cool over the next 1–2 months. Huatai’s base case is for another hike in December. The Fed’s 2027 path will hinge on incoming economic data—especially whether the labour market sustains improvement that drives wage growth and on subsequent inflation dynamics.

【CITIC Securities sees one more 25bps Fed hike this year】CITIC Securities says the Fed raised 25bps in September as expected and upgraded its growth and inflation forecasts; the dot plot and Fed remarks were hawkish. Strong market expectations made the September hike the path of least resistance. The timing and size of further hikes will depend heavily on oil prices, which remain unpredictable; with headline inflation YoY likely to drop notably in early next year, the case for additional tightening should weaken. CITIC expects one more 25bps hike before year-end and a likely pause next year. US financial conditions are unlikely to ease meaningfully; investors should favour assets with fundamental support rather than those that rely solely on liquidity.

【原料成本大幅攀升 炭黑市場回暖】9月16日，炭黑概念股集體異動，龍星科技、黑貓股份漲停，永東股份、聯科科技等同步走高。近期，國內炭黑市場迎來一輪爆發式上漲，主流產品報價接連突破歷史關口。截至9月16日，生意社炭黑基準價報12778.57元/噸，較月初（9142.8元/噸）上漲39.77%。“受主焦煤、配煤等上游原煤供給收緊、煤價堅挺影響，焦化企業生產成本居高不下，持續陷入虧損狀態。為控制虧損幅度、最佳化生產效益，多數焦化企業主動壓減生產負荷、延長裝置檢修週期，行業整體開工率大幅下滑。”有業內人士記者表示。產能收縮直接導致煤焦油副產品產出量銳減，市場商品流通貨源大幅縮減，現貨庫存持續低位，煤焦油價格開啟快速跳漲模式、一路衝高。原料成本的大幅攀升，抬高了炭黑企業的整體生產成本，形成強有力的成本倒逼漲價邏輯，為炭黑漲價提供了核心支撐。（證券日報）

### 2026-09-17 07:53:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Interest Rate #Importance 1

HKMA Lifts Base Rate to 4.25% Following Fed Hike — The Hong Kong Monetary Authority (HKMA) raised its base rate by 25bps to 4.25% on September 17, 2026, marking its first hike since 2023 and following the U.S. Fed’s decision to lift its federal funds target range for the first time in three years. The move reflects Hong Kong’s Linked Exchange Rate System, which keeps the Hong Kong dollar within a 7.75–7.85 per U.S. dollar trading band and requires local interest rates to broadly track U.S. monetary policy, regardless of domestic economic conditions. The HKMA’s move came as Hong Kong’s economy continues to benefit from robust trade, although higher borrowing costs could weigh on a property market only recently emerging from a prolonged downturn. Sentiment may also remain cautious amid concerns over China’s efforts to curb mainland capital outflows. Meanwhile, GDP grew 4.3% yoy in Q2 2026, slowing from 5.9% in Q1 as household consumption and fixed investment growth eased, while government spending remained subdued.

【KEVIN WARSH holds 30-minute press conference, shortest since 2011】Federal Reserve Chair KEVIN WARSH held a roughly 30-minute post-meeting press conference Wednesday, the shortest since routine chair briefings began in 2011. He stuck to a pledge not to pre-announce Fed moves, confining comments to this week's meeting and officials' current economic views. The Fed reordered press-room seating alphabetically by outlet, moving AFP to the front and shifting Wall Street Journal and Refinitiv reporters rearward. A special working group Warsh created is reviewing the Fed's communications, including press conferences, leaving open the possibility the briefing format could be changed or discontinued.

【碳酸鋰期價大幅回撥 產業鏈承壓短期供需料維持緊平衡】進入9月，本該迎來傳統“金九銀十”旺季的碳酸鋰市場卻走出一輪單邊下行走勢，帶動鋰礦板塊上市公司股價同步走弱。9月16日，國內碳酸鋰期貨主力合約價格盤中一度跌至124020元/噸，9月以來累計下跌逾兩成。回溯至今年5月份，國內碳酸鋰期貨主力合約價格最高一度在21萬元/噸左右，短短4個月跌去超四成，基本抹平年內漲幅。證券時報記者在採訪中瞭解到，本輪碳酸鋰價格下跌受到頭部電池廠下修排產傳聞、庫存統計口徑切換等市場訊息影響，或是對前期過度樂觀預期的調整。當然，也有觀點認為，美聯儲加息預期、市場對遠期需求的預期偏悲觀也是影響因素。業內人士認為，當前市場需求處於高位，碳酸鋰短期仍將維持供需緊平衡狀態，後續價格走勢需密切觀察終端需求、庫存變化及新增產能釋放情況。

【美聯儲主席沃什踐行自己的主張 召開了一場創紀錄短的記者會】美聯儲主席凱文·沃什曾表示，政策制定者應該少說話。週三他在例行政策會議後舉行了有記錄以來美聯儲主席最簡短的記者會，以此踐行了自己的建議。沃什在宣佈三年多來首次加息後舉行的例行記者會僅持續了約30分鐘。這是自2011年美聯儲主席開始定期舉行新聞釋出會以來最短的一次。這位主席堅持不預測美聯儲未來的行動，而是專注於本週的會議以及美聯儲官員目前對經濟的看法。美聯儲還調整了簡報室的座位安排。以往各大報紙和通訊社的記者都坐在前排，現在座位改為按媒體名稱字母順序排列。這意味著法新社的記者被安排在了前排，而《華爾街日報》和路透的記者則被安排在了房間後排。另外記者會的未來仍懸而未決。沃什設立的五個特別工作組之一將負責審查溝通機制。該工作組的任務是評估美聯儲如何在“不確定性中傳達政策審議和決定”，這一行動可能包括導致記者會以及其他溝通工具可能被取消。

### 2026-09-17 07:26:51  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Extends Decline as Supply Concerns Ease — Brent crude fell to around $105.5 per barrel on Thursday, extending losses for a second consecutive session following reports that Saudi Arabia plans to restore roughly half the capacity of its East-West pipeline within days and bring it back to full operation within six weeks. The key pipeline, which provides an alternative route around the Strait of Hormuz, was damaged in drone attacks last week. In the meantime, Saudi Arabia has stepped up efforts to transport more crude through Hormuz with assistance from the US military. Meanwhile, US Energy Secretary Chris Wright said that 18 million barrels of crude and petroleum products passed through Hormuz earlier this week. In the US, official data showed that crude inventories declined by 640,000 barrels to 423.4 million barrels, although the decrease was smaller than analysts had anticipated. The figures differed sharply from the API’s earlier estimate of a 7.1 million-barrel increase in crude inventories.

【美國眾議院將員工持股估值法案送交特朗普簽署】一項旨在明確員工持股計劃估值標準的兩黨法案已在美國眾議院獲得透過，現已送交美國總統特朗普簽署生效。眾議院週三以 401 票贊成、14 票反對的表決結果透過《透過持股實現退休法案》（S.2403）（Retire Through Ownership Act）。該投票結果凸顯國會對員工持股計劃（ESOP）的支援，在華盛頓黨派對立的大環境下，推動員工持股成為罕見的兩黨共識領域。法案將解決 “充分對價” 問題，這也是近期多起員工持股計劃相關訴訟的核心爭議點。法案一經簽署生效，將允許員工持股計劃受託人聘請獨立評估師，依據美國國稅局《收入裁定 59-60》開展估值。該裁定是美國國稅局出於稅務核算、股權轉讓及法律糾紛處理目的，用於確定非上市私營企業股份公允市場價值的指導檔案。

【香港跟隨美聯儲加息，為三年多來首次】香港金融管理局跟隨美國聯邦儲備委員會加息，這是 2023 年以來首次加息。香港金管局週四釋出宣告，將基準利率上調 25 個基點至 4.25%。香港金管局同步跟進美聯儲政策，以維護港元與美元的聯絡匯率制度。市場當前關注香港各大銀行（包括滙豐控股與渣打集團）將於當日晚些時候公佈的調整方案，其中包括最優惠貸款利率的變動。在香港，最優惠貸款利率是銀行按揭貸款的定價基準，因此即便基準利率收緊，銀行仍可透過調整該利率影響信貸與借貸需求。

### 2026-09-17 07:08:45  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Edges Higher After GDP Data — The NZX 50 rose 8 points, or 0.1%, to 13,631 in Thursday morning trade, extending gains from the previous session following the release of New Zealand’s GDP data. Fresh data showed that New Zealand’s economy grew more than expected, with GDP rising 2.6% year-on-year, while easing to 0.2% quarter-on-quarter, amid elevated oil prices due to the ongoing Middle East conflict. However, the Fed’s decision to hike interest rates by 25 bps limited the gains. Traders continued to monitor developments in the Iran-US war and its impact on oil prices. Financials, healthcare, and utilities mainly drove the index, with top movers including Channel Infrastructure (1.5%), Freightways Group (0.7%), AFT Pharmaceuticals (0.5%), EBOS Group (0.5%), and Gentrack Group (0.5%).

### 2026-09-17 07:00:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

New Zealand Q2 GDP Annual Growth Hits 3-Year High — New Zealand’s economy expanded by 2.6% year-on-year in the three months to June 2026, following an upwardly revised 1.7% increase in the previous quarter and exceeding expectations of 2.3% growth. It was the fourth consecutive quarter of annual growth and the fastest pace since Q2 2023, as all sectors expanded. Goods-producing industries expanded faster (4.4% vs 0.1% in Q1), boosted by manufacturing (5.4%), construction (3.7%), and electricity, gas, water, and waste services (3.6%). Service industries grew 2.1% (unchanged from Q1), due mainly to higher wholesale trade (7.4%), public administration and safety (4.0%), retail trade and accommodation (2.6%), and healthcare and social assistance (2.4%). Primary industries also continued to grow (0.3% vs 0.6%), driven by agriculture, forestry, and fishing (1.7%). On a quarterly basis, the economy advanced 0.2% in Q2, slowing from an upwardly revised 0.9% growth in Q1 but surpassing forecasts of 0.1%.

### 2026-09-17 06:57:01  #公司

【從數量擴張到質量篩選，併購重組市場新動向受關注】近期，包括焦作萬方、金橙子、普路通在內的多家上市公司對外發布終止重大資產重組相關公告，引發市場關注。以普路通為代表的部分公司釋出終止公告後，股價出現明顯回撥。從終止重大資產重組的原因看，外部市場環境發生變化、交易雙方關鍵核心條款未達成一致是近期重組終止的兩大主因，其中不乏披露籌劃重組意向後不久便宣佈終止交易的案例。受訪專家認為，併購重組終止不是“利空一刀切”，影響要分主體、分原因看。終止案例密集出現的背後，交易雙方對標的質量、估值作價與產業協同的審視明顯趨嚴，併購市場有望從數量擴張轉入更充分的價格博弈與質量篩選階段。（中證報）

【U.S. House passes Russia sanctions bill, grants president tariff authority on Russian oil and gas imports】The U.S. House passed a Russia sanctions-and-tariffs bill 262-159; the measure will be sent to President Trump for signature. The bill targets Russian officials, companies and financial institutions that support the war in Ukraine and authorizes the president to impose tariffs on major importers of Russian oil and gas. Tariff authority was a key Democratic objection; Democrats warned it could allow the president to designate any country as aiding evasion and impose tariffs of up to 100% without oversight or a sunset clause.

### 2026-09-17 06:43:38  #市場

【私募五大策略業績分化，量化多頭領跑】8月A股市場震盪反彈，帶動私募產品淨值普遍修復。第三方機構私募排排網資料顯示，截至8月31日，在該平臺有業績記錄的12597只私募產品8月平均收益率達4.53%，今年以來的平均收益率提升至4.99%，超六成產品實現正收益。五大策略表現顯著分化：8月股票策略以5.44%的平均收益率領跑，多資產策略、組合基金策略、期貨及衍生品策略的平均收益率分別為3.84%、3.47%和3.14%，債券策略當月的平均收益率為0.44%。 更值得關注的是股票策略內部的結構性分化——量化多頭單月平均收益率達9.71%，年內收益率由負轉正至6.62%，而主觀多頭8月平均收益率僅3.82%。有私募人士表示，這種分化的本質是各策略貝塔來源不同，並非簡單的“誰強誰弱”。（中證報）

### 2026-09-17 05:37:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Brazil Central Bank Cuts Selic to 13.75% — Brazil’s central bank cut its benchmark interest rate by 25 bps to 13.75% at its September meeting, in line with market expectations. The bank noted that the external environment remains uncertain due to the lack of clarity surrounding armed conflicts in the Middle East and uncertainty over monetary policy in some advanced economies. Domestically, indicators released since the previous meeting point to a gradual moderation in economic activity, particularly in more cyclical sectors, although activity remains resilient and the labor market remains tight. Recent data show that headline inflation and the average of underlying measures have slowed, standing below the upper bound of the tolerance interval but still above the target. The Copom reiterated that the current scenario, marked by significantly higher uncertainty, deanchored inflation expectations, and elevated risks around the baseline scenario, calls for caution.

### 2026-09-17 04:40:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Canada 10-Year Yield Nears 3.94% — Canada’s 10-year government bond yield settled near 3.94% in mid-September after falling as low as 3.87% on lower oil prices. The yield then rose following the Federal Reserve’s rate hike, as the Fed raised the target range for the federal funds rate by 25 bps to 3.75%-4%. Updated Fed projections showed that most policymakers expect another hike before the end of 2026. The Bank of Canada kept its key policy rate unchanged at 2.25% at its September meeting, as widely expected. However, it noted that inflation risks had increased, while new tariffs had made the growth outlook more uncertain. Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated. Still, a halt in the oil rally following reports of a potential recovery of Saudi Arabia’s key East-West pipeline helped to slow global yields.

US TIC Inflows Reach $83.7 Billion in July — Net foreign acquisitions of long-term securities, short-term US securities and banking flows resulted in a net TIC inflow of $83.7 billion in July 2026. Net foreign private inflows totaled $73.5 billion, while net foreign official inflows reached $10.2 billion. Foreign residents increased their holdings of long-term US securities, with net purchases of $40.6 billion, including net sales of $3.7 billion by private foreign investors and net purchases of $44.4 billion by foreign official institutions. US residents increased their holdings of long-term foreign securities, with net purchases of $68.5 billion. After adjustments, overall net foreign sales of long-term securities were estimated at $27.9 billion. Foreign residents also increased their holdings of US Treasury bills by $38.8 billion, while holdings of all dollar-denominated short-term US securities and other custody liabilities rose by $65 billion.

### 2026-09-17 03:58:56  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Currency #Importance 1

Canadian Dollar Weakens After Fed Hike — The Canadian dollar weakened to near 1.40 per USD in mid-September, its lowest level in more than a month after the US Federal Reserve raised interest rates as expected. The Fed raised the target range for the federal funds rate by 25 bps to 3.75%-4%, reinforcing the US dollar’s advantage over the loonie. Updated Fed projections showed that most policymakers expect another hike before the end of 2026, widening the interest-rate differential between the US and Canada. The Bank of Canada kept its key policy rate unchanged at 2.25% at its September meeting, as widely expected. However, it noted that inflation risks had increased, while new tariffs had made the growth outlook more uncertain. The BoC left the future path of monetary policy open, saying it would adjust policy as needed. Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated.

【Wash's 28-minute press conference; predecessors averaged 54 minutes】A 2021 Federal Reserve study covering the Bernanke, Yellen and Powell eras found FOMC press conferences averaged about 54 minutes total, with roughly 10 minutes of opening remarks, about 44 minutes of media Q&A and an average of ~22 questions including follow-ups. A separate Fed study noted press conferences that begin ~30 minutes after the FOMC statement typically run about one hour. Wash's first press conference after taking office in 2026 lasted about 42 minutes; his latest in September ran roughly 28 minutes.

### 2026-09-17 03:42:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kuwait #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Kuwait Central Bank Holds Key Rate at 3.50% — The Central Bank of Kuwait held its key discount rate at 3.50% in September 2026, stating that current data reflected “the soundness and strength of monetary and financial stability” in the country. Gulf oil and gas exporters generally follow the Fed’s lead on rate moves, as most regional currencies are pegged to the US dollar. The Kuwaiti dinar is the exception, as it is pegged to a basket of currencies that includes the dollar. Most central banks in the Gulf Cooperation Council raised key interest rates following the US Federal Reserve’s decision to hike. Kuwait’s economy is expected to contract 8.1% this year, as the Iran war disrupted energy shipments through the effective closure of the Strait of Hormuz, damaged energy production facilities, and reduced traffic, affecting tourism and business across the Gulf region.

### 2026-09-17 03:34:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

US 10-Year Yield Rises to 19-Year High — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note rose to above 5.01% on Wednesday, the highest in 19 years, after the Federal Reserve raised interest rates by 25bps. The move was largely expected by financial markets after recent data pointed to high inflation, low unemployment, and strong consumer spending. Projections by FOMC members pointed to one or two additional rate hikes by next year, consistent with upward revisions to forecasts on inflation and downward revisions to unemployment. Besides higher inflation due to soaring energy prices, the Chairman Warsh also acknowledged that Treasuries have been pressured by competing for capital allocation with soaring corporate debt supply. Still, longer-term maturities rose less than the shorter part of the curve as confidence that the Fed unanimously raised rates to fight inflation restored some credibility for the central bank, after Chairman Warsh had earlier downplayed the urgency of a higher funds rate to reign in price growth.

US Stocks Swing Sharply Lower — US stocks swung lower on Wednesday after the Federal Reserve raised interest rates by 25bps, as expected. The S&P 500 fell 1% and the Dow dropped 1.5%, while the Nasdaq 100 fell 0.5%. Median projections by FOMC members reflected one or two additional hikes expected for this year, aligned with upward revisions to projections on inflation and GDP growth, in tandem with the view of lower unemployment for the upcoming years. In the meantime, FOMC Chairman Warsh struck a hawkish rhetoric by prioritizing the fight against inflation and reiterating that current gauges remain high, a reversal from previous speeches that signaled action against prices while downplaying the funds rate as preferred policy tool. Credit-sensitive sectors fell sharply, with Bank of America, Wells Fargo, and Citi falling 4%. AI hyperscalers also retreated with Microsoft, Alphabet, and losing more than 1%.

### 2026-09-17 03:26:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Oman #Interest Rate #Importance 0

Oman Central Bank Raises Key Interest Rate — The Central Bank of Oman (CBO) raised its key repo rate by 25 bps to 4.50% in September 2026, mirroring the Federal Reserve’s decision. The move followed the Fed’s announcement that it would raise the target range for the federal funds rate by 25 bps to 3.75%-4%, while a majority of FOMC members projected another hike this year. In a statement, the CBO said the decision is in line with its monetary policy framework and consistent with the Omani rial’s fixed exchange rate regime, supporting monetary and financial stability. The bank also noted that the measure is expected to help contain inflationary pressures and maintain price stability, mitigate undesirable cross-border capital flows, and support investor confidence by limiting risks associated with exchange-rate volatility.

【Kevin Warsh: I will not provide forward guidance】Kevin Warsh said he will not disclose details of future Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) rate decisions or provide forward guidance. He called today’s rate decision careful, serious and responsible, and said the Fed had been preparing and deliberating for about 110–120 days. Warsh said the decision was based on the Fed’s assessment of current conditions, employment trends and economic strength, not driven by market forces. He added that markets sometimes try to anticipate policy and that he watches market prices for information, but today’s decision was made independently.

### 2026-09-17 03:05:12  #資料 #市場 #央行 #國際

【美聯儲主席沃什新聞釋出會要點一覽】
1、利率決議：FOMC一致投票加息25個基點；本次屬於審慎決策，核心目標實現價格穩定，認為物價穩定才是經濟可持續增長的基礎。
2、通脹判斷（核心鷹派）：通脹水平過高、持續時間久；夏季資料沒有改善，大量細分項漲幅仍高於3%；委員會尚未確信通脹正及時向2%目標回落，通脹風險向上，就業市場風險大體平衡。
3、經濟與就業：美國經濟正在走強、具備韌性；當前大致實現充分就業；不需要以損害勞動力市場為代價壓制通脹，物價穩定最終有利於勞動者實際工資提升。
4、政策溝通：放棄前瞻指引：不打算給出利率前瞻指引，拒絕預判未來政策行動；沃什本人不提交點陣圖；中性利率理論概念對當下實際決策沒有操作意義。
5、金融環境判斷：委員會普遍認為，當前金融條件還談不上緊縮；美債收益率上行來源於三點：美國經濟強勁、資本開支激增、地緣政治因素。
6、資料決策邏輯：不糾結單月CPI等個別資料，重點看中長期趨勢；地緣政治的前景判斷已經發生改變；油價這類單一商品價格美聯儲無法直接干預。
7、美聯儲獨立性：堅守職責邊界，不評論其他國家央行政策；被問及和總統溝通情況時不予置評；獨立性是雙向，要把行動限定在自身使命範圍。
8、AI相關表態：密切跟蹤AI發展動向；AI工作組要在年底提交報告；但AI風險收益的政策抉擇交給其他政策制定者，美聯儲不負責AI安全監管。
9、市場後果：表態釋放鷹派訊號，交易員押注年底前還會再加息兩次；美元指數走高、美債收益率上行，黃金、美股承壓走弱。

UAE Central Bank Raises Base Rate — The Central Bank of the UAE (CBUAE) raised its overnight deposit facility base rate by 25 bps to 3.9% in September 2026, mirroring the Federal Reserve’s decision. The UAE’s monetary policy remains closely aligned with that of the Fed, as the dirham is pegged to the US dollar. The decision followed the Fed’s announcement that it would raise the interest rate on reserve balances by 25 bps to a 3.75%-4% range at its meeting, while a majority of FOMC members projected another hike this year. Ibovespa Slips Following Fed Hike — The Ibovespa edged lower to trade near the 186,000 mark on Wednesday following the US Fed’s monetary policy decision and ahead of the BCB’s. The Fed raised interest rates by 25 bps and a majority of FOMC members projected another hike this year. In contrast, the BCB is expected to announce a 25bps Selic cut after market close, marking the fifth consecutive reduction and the final Copom decision before the October presidential election. Financials were mixed, with private banks edging lower, while Banco do Brasil (+2.5%), BB Seguridade (+1.5%) and Caixa (+1%) posted gains. Petrobras weighed on the index, tumbling more than 2.5% as oil prices retreated amid reports that Saudi Arabia is offering additional crude cargoes via Oman. Miners also posted losses, with Vale and CSN Mineração down about 2%, while Usiminas fell more than 3%.

TSX Trades Near Flat After Fed Decision — The TSX traded near flat around 35,500 on Wednesday following the US Fed’s widely expected decision to raise its benchmark rate by 25 bps to a 3.75%-4% range. Major banks were mostly higher, with CIBC and National Bank up about 1%. Oil prices and sovereign yields eased after their global rally in recent weeks weighed on equity markets, as investors fear central banks will keep monetary policy tighter for longer to contain inflationary pressures from soaring energy prices. Gold prices edged lower following the FOMC decision. Miners traded mostly lower, with WPM erasing earlier gains to fall more than 1.5%. Elsewhere, European Commission President von der Leyen said the EU is opening the door for Canada to become its first “associate member,” with cooperation across sectors including manufacturing, AI, critical minerals and energy. This follows an escalation in the US-Canada trade war after trade talks collapsed last month.

Gold Pares Gains After Fed — Gold prices pared early gains to trade little changed around $4,300 an ounce on Wednesday as traders digested the Fed’s latest monetary policy decision. The central bank raised the federal funds rate by 25bps, as expected, and signalled at least one more rate hike this year to curb persistently high inflation. Meanwhile, the Bank of England is expected to leave borrowing costs unchanged tomorrow, while the Bank of Japan is set to raise rates on Friday. Last week, the ECB raised borrowing costs for the second time this year. Oil prices retreated today, but the situation in the Middle East remains fragile, keeping supply risks elevated. The continued energy shock is expected to add further pressure to inflation and could prompt additional monetary tightening.

### 2026-09-17 02:42:45  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 1

US Dollar Rises to 7-Week High — The dollar index rose past the 100 for the first time in nearly seven weeks on Wednesday after the Federal Reserve raised interest rates by 25bps. The decision was unanimous among the FOMC and expected by markets after recent data pointed to elevated gauges of underlying inflation, low unemployment, and a sharp rebound in retail sales that underscores the strong position of consumers despite higher price growth. Projections by the policy-setting committee pointed to a loose consensus that at least one other hike is warranted this year, while unemployment was revised lower and economic growth was revised higher. The decision supported the dollar after earlier remarks by Chairman Warsh raised skepticism over the Fed's willingness to fight inflation through higher policy rates instead of other tools, rekindling a dash to precious metals and other safe currencies. In the meantime, the Bank of England is expected to hold rates unchanged tomorrow, while the Bank of Japan is due to hike.

### 2026-09-17 02:26:02  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #Treasury Yields #US10Y #Impact bearish #DXY #Impact bullish #S&P500

【Preview: Wosh press conference — can he avoid repeating July communication mishap?】Markets will watch whether Wosh repairs communication after July’s vague, evasive answers; Natixis expects him to frame any rate hike as necessary to ensure inflation returns to target promptly. If the Fed raises rates as expected this week, attention will be on whether Wosh views energy- and tariff-driven inflation as one-off supply shocks or as more persistent/second-round/expectations-driven pressure—emphasizing the former would reduce perceived need for further tightening, the latter would elevate tightening risk. Reuters flags political sensitivity: Trump has pushed for cuts while markets expect hikes; watch Wosh’s response to Trump, whether he reasserts FOMC independence, avoids naming Trump, or stresses “data dependence.” Wosh has tended to avoid forward-rate guidance; after July’s confusion and a hawkish turn at Jackson Hole, markets will watch for any change in messaging or a renewed insistence he has “no tolerance” for inflation. With the 10-year Treasury yield above 5% on Sept. 14, traders will also look for any comments addressing further bond sell-offs and concerns about fiscal- or inflation-related risk premia.

### 2026-09-17 02:22:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Yield Holds Pullback from 19-Year High — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note eased to 4.95% on Wednesday from the 19-year high of 5.01% in the previous session after the Federal Reserve raised interest rates by 25bps, as expected. The move was largely expected by financial markets after evidence of high inflation in tandem with a robust labor market and retail sales aligned with Chairman Warsh's Jackson Hole signal that borrowing costs would be raised to combat price growth. Median projections by FOMC members pointed to split expectations of one or two additional rate hikes by next year, consistent with upward revisions to forecasts on inflation and downward revisions to unemployment. The yield on the longer-end of the curve eased more than the front end with the signal that the Fed heeds to higher prices. Still, yields on the 10-year note are 80bps higher since the start of the year. The impact of soaring energy inflation magnified pressure from soaring corporate debt supply and widening budget deficits.

US Stocks Hold Increase After Fed — US stocks remained mostly higher on Wednesday on a rebound from the tech sector, while markets assessed how the economy will fare with the rate hike from the Federal Reserve. The S&P 500 added 0.4% and the Nasdaq 100 gained 1%, while the Dow inched below the flatline. The Fed raised rates by 25bps, as expected, and median projections by FOMC members showed one or two additional hikes expected for this year as inflation is due to remain high and unemployment is expected to remain low. Chip producers and hardware companies extended yesterday's rebound after concerns over AI safety risks voiced by Anthropic and OpenAI jeopardized the momentum of soaring hyperscaler capital expenditure. Intel, Marvell, and Dell gained over 5%, while Nvidia and AMD were 3% higher. In turn, banks, retail, and pharmaceutical companies were mostly higher.

### 2026-09-17 02:02:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Interest Rate #Importance 3

Fed Raises Rates for 1st Time Since 2023 — The Fed unanimously raised the target range for the federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75%-4.00% in September 2026 as expected, marking the first rate hike since 2023. Policymakers noted that inflation remains elevated, and the move aims to support a more timely return to the 2% target. Updated projections showed that 16 of 18 officials see the possibility of at least one more 25bps rate hike later this year. Chair Warsh again declined to submit his forecasts. Meanwhile, the GDP is seen expanding at a slightly faster pace in 2026 (2.3% vs 2.2% in the June projection) and 2027 (2.4% vs 2.3%). PCE inflation is seen higher this year (3.7% vs 3.6%) but the forecast for 2027 was kept at 2.3%. Core inflation is also seen up in 2026 (3.4% vs 3.3%) but the forecast was left at 2.5% for 2027. The unemployment rate is now projected at 4.1% in both 2026 and 2027, down from the previous forecast of 4.3% for both years.

Gasoline Holds Steady — US gasoline futures held near $3.45 a gallon, close to their highest level in eight weeks, as concerns over feedstock supply eased after Saudi Arabia stated it would offer additional cargoes via Oman. Attacks last week by Iran-backed Houthi rebels shut down a crucial crude pipeline in Saudi Arabia that bypasses the Strait of Hormuz, which remains under a blockade. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories unexpectedly rose for a second consecutive week, increasing by 800 thousand barrels in the week ending September 11th, as refineries continued to operate at elevated capacity, albeit at a slightly lower rate of 96.8%, while delaying non-essential work. However, the approaching seasonal fall maintenance remains a threat to refined-product supplies. The data also showed that demand increased by 300 thousand barrels per day, while pump prices continued to rise.

Colombia Industrial Acitivty Falls Most Since 2025 — Colombia’s industrial production declined 2.3% year-on-year in July 2026, marking the sharpest decline since April 2025 and reversing a 4.1% gain in the previous month. The reading was well below market expectations of a 0.4% contraction, as production declined in 27 of 32 industries, including the manufacture of clothing (-14.1%), processing of meat (-8.1%), basic iron and steel (-12.6%), spinning, weaving and finishing of textiles (-17.9%), and cocoa, chocolate and confectionery (-19.2%). Meanwhile, output rose in the manufacture of soaps and detergents (8.8%) and beverages (2.3%). Real sales declined 1.8%, while employment also fell 1.4%.

Consumer prices increased 3.1% year-on-year in August, up from 2.9% in July and matching market expectations, although above the Bank of England’s 2.8% forecast. Higher motor-fuel prices, linked to the ongoing Iran conflict, and increased airfares contributed to the acceleration. Markets are now focused on the Federal Reserve’s decision later today and the Bank of England’s meeting on Thursday. Barratt Redrow surged more than 11% after reporting stronger-than-expected annual results, while Persimmon gained 6%. Banks including Standard Chartered, NatWest, Lloyds Banking Group and HSBC all rose after JPMorgan forecast stronger third-quarter trading and investment-banking revenue. On the other hand, energy majors Shell and BP fell more than 2% each.

### 2026-09-17 00:08:31  #觀點 #央行 #國際

【華爾街對沃什今日講話的預期一覽】華爾街各大銀行重點關注，若美聯儲今日加息，凱文·沃什將如何闡述後續政策路徑：
巴克萊銀行：預計沃什會強調經濟增長穩健、就業接近充分就業，且通脹水平依舊過高，大體延續此前偏鷹派的表態。
蒙特利爾銀行：認為沃什不會對後續加息作出明確承諾，將當前適度收緊政策定性為風險管控手段，以避免未來需要更大幅度加息。
美國銀行：提示沃什面臨艱難平衡。若釋放連續加息訊號，市場可能計價超過100個基點的緊縮幅度；而偏鴿派的加息表態，又會削弱市場對美聯儲2%通脹目標的信心。
花旗銀行：預計不會新增過多前瞻指引，沃什將重申通脹仍是難題，仍有工作有待完成。
德意志銀行：希望獲取還需要多大程度額外緊縮的明確資訊。沃什或將把本輪政策週期描述為，逆轉去年75個基點的風險對沖式降息，使政策迴歸具備足夠限制性的水平。
高盛：預計沃什會強調審慎評估新增經濟資料，或等待多份通脹報告出爐後，再決定下一步政策動作。
摩根大通：預計政策細節披露有限，但相比以往釋出會，沃什或將更加側重經濟資料。
野村證券：預計不會給出下一步政策的明確指引，政策走向將高度依賴月度通脹資料。
渣打銀行：市場會重點審視關稅與通脹問題，關注關稅帶來的影響是否為暫時性，以及美聯儲是否應當等待更明確證據後再進一步收緊政策。
道明銀行：認為如果美聯儲今日加息，後續大機率還有緊縮動作，沃什將面臨較大溝通壓力。
瑞銀：預計前瞻指引內容不多，但加息這一行為本身，會增強沃什鷹派言論的說服力。

CAC 40 Rises on Lower Oil Prices and Yields — The CAC 40 rose 0.6% to close at 8,141 on Wednesday as oil prices and bond yields paused after their recent surge. Oil prices fell amid signs of rising US inventories and reports that Saudi Arabia is offering additional crude cargoes via Oman. Meanwhile, yields retreated after a global rally in recent weeks weighed on equity markets, as investors fear central banks will keep monetary policy tighter for longer to contain inflationary pressures from soaring energy prices. Luxury stocks gained amid lower risk aversion, with LVMH up 1%, Hermès rising 0.8% and Kering adding 1.3%. Industrial shares also advanced, with Airbus up 1%, Safran adding 1.2%, Legrand up 1.8% and EssilorLuxottica rising 0.8%. STMicroelectronics (+2%) and Schneider Electric (+1.5%) tracked strength in Wall Street chipmakers. In contrast, TotalEnergies shed 0.7% on the oil pullback.

### 2026-09-16 23:38:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Colombia Retail Sales Growth Slows in July — Colombia’s real retail sales rose 5.3% year over year in July 2026, missing forecasts for an 11.2% increase and slowing from 14.7% growth in June. This marked the slowest growth since September 2024. Fourteen merchandise lines recorded increases in real sales, while five declined. The largest positive contributions came from motor vehicles and motorcycles, mainly for household use (21.8%), other motor vehicles and motorcycles (10.8%), and sound and video equipment and devices (25%). Declines in vehicle fuel sales (-3.6%), spare parts, components, accessories and lubricants for vehicles, mainly for household consumption (-2%), clothing and textiles (-3%), pharmaceutical and medicinal products (-0.7%), and footwear, leather goods and leather substitutes (-0.2%) limited overall retail growth.

【Ghalibaf proposes 'Strait Taylor Rule' before Fed decision, questions effectiveness of rate hikes】Iranian parliament speaker Ghalibaf posted ahead of the Fed decision a proposed "Strait Taylor Rule": i = r* + π* + 1.5(π−π*) + 0.5(y−y*) + α(SOH−SOH*) + β(BEM−BEM*), with α, β > 0. 【RABOBANK: Even if Fed damps rate‑hike expectations, dollar unlikely to see large sell‑off】RABOBANK analyst Jane Foley said the Fed may seek to dampen rate‑hike expectations in its upcoming policy decision. The bank still forecasts a 25bp hike but warns the Fed risks undermining market confidence in further tightening because markets have over‑priced policy tightening for next year. Foley said the dollar could weaken in the near term, but a large sell‑off is unlikely given external vulnerabilities elsewhere — notably limited UK fiscal space and the euro area’s status as an energy importer. RABOBANK sees EUR/USD trading around 1.16 over the next 1–3 months.

### 2026-09-16 23:04:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Colombia Consumer Confidence Falls in August — Colombia’s Consumer Confidence Index fell 5.2 percentage points to 15.5% in August 2026 from July. The decline was driven by a 7.7-point drop in the Consumer Expectations Index to 22.6% and a 1.6-point decrease in the Economic Conditions Index to 4.8%. Consumer expectations deteriorated as households’ assessment of their economic outlook fell to 36.4% from 41.8%, while general expectations for the economy declined to 16.7% from 24.8%. The outlook for the country’s economy fell to 14.8% from 24.3%. Meanwhile, households’ assessment of current economic conditions declined to 5.4% from 11.1%. Willingness to buy durable goods increased to 4.2% from 1.7%. Compared with August 2025, the Consumer Confidence Index increased by 17.9 percentage points.

【Venezuela to ship about 15,000 tonnes of Venalum aluminium to US; departure expected Friday】Venezuela is preparing to export about 15,000 tonnes of aluminium to the United States under agreements with Mocorí Energy Group and private miner Heeney Capital, according to foreign media and sources. The cargo, produced by state-owned Venalum, would be the first shipment to the US in years and is scheduled to depart Venezuela on Friday. The move is part of Caracas’s push to diversify its economy away from oil as commercial ties with the US rapidly normalize. US officials may announce the shipment this week during the G20 energy summit in Houston.

### 2026-09-16 22:51:09  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Crude Falls Toward $106 — Brent crude fell to around $106 a barrel on Wednesday, extending its retreat after two sessions of gains as the US administration sought to ease concerns over disruptions to Saudi oil supplies. US Energy Secretary Chris Wright said Saudi Arabia’s damaged East-West pipeline should resume operations within days, describing the outage as temporary. However, independent analysts warned that repairs could take several weeks. While the pipeline remains offline, Saudi Arabia has moved to increase crude exports through the Strait of Hormuz with US military assistance. Meanwhile, US government data showed crude inventories fell by 640,000 barrels to 423.4 million barrels in the week ended September 11, although the decline was smaller than analysts had expected. Stocks at the Cushing, Oklahoma, hub also decreased. The data contrasted sharply with the American Petroleum Institute’s previous estimate of a 7.1 million-barrel increase in crude inventories.
